Let's derive `project_simplex` accurately.

```
def project_simplex(z, S):
    n = len(z)
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float).copy()
    z = np.clip(z, -1e9, 1e9)
    lo = float(np.min(z) - 1.0)
    hi = float(np.max(z) + 1.0)
    for _ in range(80):
        mid=(lo+hi)/2
        s=np.sum(np.clip(z-mid,0.0,1.0))
        if s > S:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    h=np.clip(z-(lo+hi)/2,0.0,1.0)
    # fix residual
    err=S - np.sum(h)
    if err > 1e-15:
       # add to smallest h? But capacity at most 1-h; choose first where add err <=1-h
       idx=np.argmax(1.0 - h)
       add=min(err, 1.0-h[idx])
       h[idx]+=add
       err-=add
       if err>1e-15:
           # distribute
           for i in range(n):
              cap=1.0-h[i]
              if cap>1e-15:
                  add=min(err,cap); h[i]+=add; err-=add; if err<=1e-15 break
    elif err < -1e-15:
       idx=np.argmin(h)
       remove=min(-err, h[idx]); h[idx]-=remove; err+=remove
       if err<-1e-15:
          for i in range(n):
             if h[i]>1e-15:
                rem=min(-err,h[i]); h[i]-=rem; err+=rem; if err>=-1e-15 break
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # final residual to a midpoint
    err=S - np.sum(h)
    if abs(err)>1e-15:
       # choose index with capacity opposite
       if err>0:
          idx=np.argmax(1-h)
       else:
          idx=np.argmin(h)
       h[idx]+=err
       h[idx]=min(max(h[idx],0),1)
    return h
```
Bisection 80 over range up to maybe 1; good. But if S outside [0,n] not possible; here S=n/2.

Potential problem if z has large values due to gradient: min could be -1e8; bisection range huge 80 iterations enough? 80 halves 1e8 to 1e-7 okay. Use 100. Or sort method more exact.

Sort method for projection with [0,1]: Find t such that sum(max(0,min(1,z-t)))=S. Could binary bisection enough.

Smooth gradient finite difference:
```
def smooth_obj(h,n,T):
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
    vals = (2.0/n)*corr
    if T <= 0: return float(np.max(vals))
    m = float(np.max(vals))

Soft objective function:
```
def soft_objective(h,n,T):
    v = (2.0/n)*np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
    m = float(np.max(v))
    if T <= 1e-12:
        return m
    # v may have values slightly >m due to? m max.
    e = np.exp((v - m)/T)
    return m + T*float(np.sum? log sum)
```

def soft_grad_analytic(h,n,T):
    v = dx*corr
    m = max(v)
    if T <= 1e-12: active = v>=m-1e-12; weights=active/active.sum()
    else: ew = exp((v-m)/T); wsum=sum(ew); weights=ew/wsum
    g=zeros
    # center

    for a in range(1,n):
       wm=weights[n-1-a]; wp=weights[n-1+a]

```
def soft_step(h,n,T, max_time, initial_step=0.5):
  start=time.time()
  f,_ = soft_objective(h,n,T)
  step = initial_step
  for it in range(max_iter):
    if time.time()-start>max_time: break
    g=soft_grad_analytic(h,n,T)
    norm=np.linalg.norm(g)
    if norm<1e-9: break
    best_f=f; best_h=h; best_step=step
    # Try a few multipliers of current step: 1, 0.5, 2
    for mult in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,2.0,4.0] maybe sorted current? 
       eta = step*mult
       h_cand = project_simplex(h - eta*g, n/2)
       f_cand = soft_objective(h_cand,n,T)
       if f_cand < best_f - 1e-12:
          best_f=f_cand; best_h=h_cand; best_step=step*mult
    if best_h is not h:
       h=best_h; f=best_f; step=best_step maybe*1.1 if improved
    else:
       step *= 0.5
       if step<1e-6: break
```

def anneal_exact(h,n,time_limit,T_start=0.02,T_end=0.0001,best_h,best_c):
  c=eval(h)
  T = T_start
  cool = (T_end/T_start)**(1/max_steps)
  it=0
  while time...:
    it+=1
    # propose
    ...
    cand=project/pair; c_new=eval(cand)
    if c_new < c*(1-1e-12) or random < exp(-(c_new-c)/T):
       h=cand; c=c_new
       if c < best_c: best=...
    T *= cool
  return h,best
```

Pseudo:
```
import time, math
import numpy as np

BEST = None? maybe inside run.

def exact_c5(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    n=len(h)
    if n==0: return inf
    corr=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    return float(np.max(corr)*2.0/n)

def project_simplex(z,S): ...

def make_feasible(h,n): return project_simplex(np.asarray(h,float), n/2)

def soft_vals(h,n): return (2.0/n)*np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')

def soft_objective(h,n,T):
    vals=soft_vals(h,n)
    m=max
    if T<=0 return m
    return m + T*np.log(np.sum(np.exp((vals-m)/T)))

def soft_grad(h,n,T):
    vals=...
    m=...
    if T<=0:
       active=vals>=m-1e-10
       weights=active/active.sum() if any
    else:
       ew=np.exp((vals-m)/T); sum = ...; weights=ew/s
    g=np.zeros(n)
    # use tol maybe skip tiny weights
    # center
    wc=weights[n-1]
    if wc>1e-10: g += wc*(1-2h)
    for a in range(1,n):
       wm=weights[n-1-a]
       wp=weights[n-1+a]
       if wm>...:
          g[:-a] += wm*(1-h[a:])
          g[a:] -= wm*h[:-a]
       if wp>...:
          g[a:] += wp*(1-h[:-a])  # Wait plus d=a: grad[i]=1-h[j] for i=j+a -> g[a:] += 1-h[:n-a]; g[:n-a] -= h[a:] . Correct.
          g[:-a] -= wp*h[a:]  # Note if using g[a:] and g[:-a] slices, but g[a:] includes indices from a to end; g[:-a] excludes last a. No overlap for a>0. Good.
    return g
```
Need be careful with slice assignment: In Python, `g[:-a] += ...` for a=1 works length n-1. For a=n-1, `g[:-n+1]` length1, `g[n-1:]` length1. Good. For a maybe n? no.

For plus: `g[a:] += wp*(1.0 - h[:n-a]); g[:n-a] -= wp*h[a:]`. For minus: `g[:n-a] += wm*(1.0 - h[a:]); g[a:] -= wm*h[:n-a]`. Good.

But if weights for many lages, adding to slices repeatedly O(n^2) arithmetic; fine.

Exact line search for maximize? We can use `smooth_optimize` as described. Need avoid infinite if gradient sign wrong. We can check gradient direction: compute f0; try h - step*g. If not decrease, maybe try +? But gradient should descend. If mapping wrong, local search will fail; but parent fallback. We can include a finite-difference check in `run` of analytic gradient to avoid using wrong gradient:
```
def gradient_check_ok(n=12, T=0.05):
  rng = np.random.default_rng(123)
  h = project_simplex(rng.random(n), n/2)
  f0=soft_objective(h,n,T)
  g=soft_grad(h,n,T)
  eps=1e-6
  fd=np.zeros(n)
  for i in range(n):
    h1=h.copy(); h1[i]+=eps; f1=soft_objective(h1,n,T)
    h2=h.copy(); h2[i]-=eps; f2=soft_objective(h2,n,T)
    fd[i]=(f1-f2)/(2*eps)
  if np.max(np.abs(g-fd)) < 1e-3 * max(1,np.max(np.abs(fd))): return True else False
```
But if T=0.05, smooth. This check consumes maybe 24 evals for n=12, negligible. Use top-level no closures. However `np.random.default_rng` available. If check fails, optimize with finite difference gradient: implement `soft_grad_fd(h,n,T,eps=1e-5)`. Good.

Finite difference gradient:
```
def soft_grad_fd(h,n,T,eps=1e-6):
  g=np.zeros(n)
  f0? not needed
  for i in range(n):
    hp=h.copy(); hp[i]+=eps
    hm=h.copy(); hm[i]-=eps
    g[i]=(soft_objective(hp,n,T)-soft_objective(hm,n,T))/(2*eps)
  return g
```
This allows values outside [0,1] but smooth objective defined. Could be okay. But if outside, 1-h negative, corr can be negative, values maybe negative; logsumexp shift still okay. But we only evaluate near feasible, eps small.

def smooth_optimize(h,n,time_limit, use_analytic=True, seed=0):
    h=project(h,n/2); c=exact; best_c=c; best_h=h.copy(); t0=time.time()
    T_list = [0.2, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.004, 0.001]
    # maybe start fewer if time
    for T in T_list:
       if time > limit: break
       # initial step based on gradient norm; use eta base 1.0? 
       f=soft_objective(h,n,T)
       step=1.0
       max_iter per T maybe 400
       for it in range(500):
          if time > limit*? break? Need allocate per T.
          if time.time()-t0 > time_limit: break
          g=grad_func(h,n,T)
          norm=np.linalg.norm(g)
          if norm<1e-12: break
          # Candidate base values: [0.5,1,2,5,10,20] scaled by 1/norm? But step not base? Try directional step size s = base/(norm +1e-12)
          best_f=f; best_candidate=None; best_base=1
          for base in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0,5.0]:
             eta=base/(norm+1e-12)
             cand=project(h - eta*g, S)
             f_c=soft_objective(cand,n,T)
             if f_c < best_f - 1e-10: best...
          if best_candidate is not None:
             h=best_candidate; f=best_f; # keep base adjust
          else:
             # try smaller bases [0.01,0.005]
             ... if still no: breaks after some
       c_new=exact(h,n)
       if c_new < best_c: best...
    return best_h,best_c
```

def anneal_exact(h,n,time_limit,seed=2,Tstart=0.01,Tend=1e-5, max_iter=...):
  h=project; c=exact; best...; t0
  T=Tstart
  # estimate max_steps maybe time/0.02? Use loop
  no_improve=0
  num_pairs = n*(n-1)/2
  while time<limit:
    # choose hot loop of 1000 proposals to avoid per-proposal time check overhead
    for _ in range(1000):
       i,j = rng.integers(0,n,2)
       if i==j: continue
       low = max(-h[i], h[j]-1); high = min(1-h[i], h[j])
       if high - low < 1e-12: continue
       # choose delta: sample 2-3 values
       for mode in range(3):
          if mode==0: d = low + (high-low)*rng.random()
          elif high>0 and low<0: d = (rng.random()-0.5)*(high-low)*0.5 # small?
          else: d = low if rng.random()<0.5 else high
          if abs(d)<1e-12: continue
          cand = h.copy(); cand[i]+=d; cand[j]-=d
          # if bounds okay after rounding, no project; else project
          if cand.min()< -1e-12 or cand.max()>1+1e-12: cand=project(cand,S)
          cn=exact(cand)
          if cn < c or (cn-c)>? accept random
              if cn < c or rng.random() < math.exp(-(cn-c)/T if T>0 else -inf):
                  h=cand; c=cn; break # maybe accept one per pair proposal
    # update T cool
    factor = (Tend/Tstart)**(1/num_batches)
    T = max(Tend, T*factor)
    if c < best_c:
       best...
       no=0
    else:
       no += 1
       if no > 20: break
  return best
```

else keep
```

```
def ensure_feasible(h,n):
  h=np.array(h,float)
  S=n/2
  err=S - sum(h)
  if abs(err)>1e-9:
      # project
      return project_simplex(h,S)
  else:
      # adjust one coordinate if out of bounds or err tiny
      h=np.clip(h,0,1)
      # if clip changes sum, project
      if abs((S-sum(h)))>1e-9: return project_simplex(h,S)
      # add err to index with capacity
      if abs(err)>0:
         idx = argmax(1-h) if err>0 else argmin(h)
         if abs(err)<= (1-h[idx])? for err positive; add err
         h[idx]+=err
         h=np.clip(h,0,1)
      return h
```
If parent sum exactly but floating max maybe, use exact. If projection from small clipping changes C, we can compare with exact and if projected C > original evaluated before project? def resample_h(h0,n):
  m=len(h0)
  if m==n: return h0
  x0=(np.arange(m)+0.5)*2.0/m

  return out

Now let's refine `soft_grad` for T small and weights: If T extremely small, exp((vals-m)/T) under/over. Use if any vals close. Weights sum may underflow for all non-max to zero; fine. For T <=0, active average.
```
def soft_grad(h,n,T):
    vals = (2.0/n)*np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
    m = float(np.max(vals))
    if T <= 0:
       active = vals >= m - 1e-12
       if not np.any(active): active[n-1?]=?; active = vals.argmax()==1
       weights = active / active.sum()
    else:
       shifted = (vals-m)/T
       # to avoid underflow, max shifted=0, min very negative; exp okay
       ew = np.exp(np.clip(shifted, -745, 0.0))
       s=ew.sum()
       if s <=0: weights=zeros; weights[argmax(vals)]=1
       else weights=ew/s
    g=np.zeros(n,float)
    # if n==1? S=0.5? n could be 1? We won't use. handle.
    if n == 1: return g
    wc=weights[n-1]
    if abs(wc)>1e-12: g += wc*(1 - 2*h)
    # We can optimize by only loop over weights > 1e-10? For speed, precompute active list? But n small.
    for a in range(1,n):
       wm=weights[n-1-a]
       wp=weights[n-1+a]
       if wm>1e-10:
           g[:-a] += wm*(1.0 - h[a:])
           g[a:] -= wm*h[:-a]
       if wp>1e-10:
           g[a:] += wp*(1.0 - h[:-a])
           g[:n-a] -= wp*h[a:]
    return g
```
But weights sum to 1, and T small one weight ~1; loop over all a still adds zeros for most. Could optimize by finding nonzero weights indices: `nonzero = np.where(weights>1e-10)[0]`, then for p in nonzero determine a,d. This is faster for small T. Use:
```
idxs = np.nonzero(weights > 1e-10)[0]
for p in idxs:
   w=weights[p]
   if p == n-1: ...
   elif p < n-1: a=n-1-p; minus
   else: a=p-(n-1); plus
```
This avoids n loop for T small. For T large, maybe many; still n≤240. Use this.
Need for T large (0.2) weights all nonzero ~1/length; active all. Then loop 2n-1. Fine.

```
def gradient_check(n=13, T=0.05):
   rng=np.random.default_rng(12345)
   h=project_simplex(rng.random(n), n/2.0)
   f0=soft_objective(h,n,T)
   g=soft_grad(h,n,T)
   eps=1e-5
   fd=np.zeros(n)
   for i in range(n):
      hp=h.copy(); hp[i]+=eps; hm=h.copy(); hm[i]-=eps
      fd[i]=(soft_objective(hp,n,T)-soft_objective(hm,n,T))/(2*eps)
   scale = max(1.0, float(np.max(np.abs(fd))))
   return float(np.max(np.abs(g-fd))) <= 1e-3 * scale
```

Pseudo for smooth_optimize with gradient method param:
```
def smooth_optimize(h,n,time_limit,grad_func,seed=0):
    h=ensure_feasible(h,n)
    S=n/2
    c=exact_c5(h)
    best_c=c; best_h=h.copy()
    t0=time.time()
    T_list = [0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.004,0.001]
    # if time_limit small, maybe fewer: if <30 use [0.1,0.03,0.005]
    if time_limit < 60: T_list=[0.1,0.03,0.005]
    for T in T_list:
       if elapsed>time_limit: break
       f=soft_objective(h,n,T)
       # choose max iterations per T by time slice? Let it run until next threshold? We need not spend all on first T.
       # allocate max_iter = 800 for first, but loop checks a subtime? Could use elapsed but T schedule consumes all in first? Need split: per_T_time = time_limit / len(T_list)
       per_t = time_limit / len(T_list)
       tp_start = time.time()
       step_base = 1.0
       stall=0
       while elapsed < time_limit and time.time()-tp_start < per_t:
          g=grad_func(h,n,T)
          gn=np.linalg.norm(g)
          if gn < 1e-12: break
          d = -g/gn
          best_f=f; best_cand=None; best_base=1.0
          bases = [0.01,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0]
          # direction norm 1, alpha is magnitude. Objective values ~0.4, f derivative along g? using d norm 1, derivative maybe gn. alpha small.
          for alpha in bases:
             cand = project_simplex(h + alpha*d, S)
             fc = soft_objective(cand,n,T)
             if fc < best_f - 1e-10:
                best_f=fc; best_cand=cand; best_base=alpha
          if best_cand is None:
             # try smaller/larger? [0.002,0.005,2]
             for alpha in [0.002,0.005,2.0]:
                cand=project_simplex(h+alpha*d,S); fc=soft_objective(cand,n,T)
                if fc<best_f-1e-10: ...
          if best_cand is not None:
             h=best_cand; f=best_f; stall=0
          else:
             # random small projected step to escape soft local
             if stall < 20:
                 alpha = 0.02
                 rng = np.random.default_rng(12345+int(time.time()*1000)%100000)? Can't use time in seed? okay deterministic? Use static seed + iter.
                 direction = rng.normal(size=n)
                 cand=project_simplex(h + alpha*direction, S)
                 fc=soft_objective(cand,n,T)
                 if fc < f:
                    h=cand; f=fc
             stall += 1
             if stall > 30: break
       c_new=exact_c5(h)
       if c_new < best_c - 1e-12:
          best_c=c_new; best_h=h.copy()
    # final exact refine after smooth maybe? We'll do outside.
    return best_h,best_c
```

```
def project_simplex_fast(z,S):
    h=np.clip(z,0,1)
    diff=S - sum(h)
    if abs(diff) <= 1e-12: return h
    # distribute diff to indices with room, proportional to room? 
    if diff>0:
       room=1-h
       idx = np.where(room>1e-9)[0]
       if len(idx)==0: return h # infeasible
       # add to all rooms proportionally: add = diff * room[idx]/sum(room[idx]) but may exceed room? if diff<=sum(room)
       frac = diff / sum(room[idx])
       h[idx] += diff * (room[idx]/sum(room[idx])) ??? This is diff distribution by room, but if frac? Actually simply if we want not exceed 1, h_idx += diff * room_idx / sum_room. Since diff <= total room, this adds less than room to each? If room_i/sum_room * diff <= room_i iff diff <= sum_room. yes. Good.
    else add negative from h similarly.
    h=np.clip(h,0,1); fix residual.
```

Define:
```
def project_simplex(z,S):
    z=np.asarray(z,float)
    n=len(z)
    h=np.clip(z,0.0,1.0)
    s=float(np.sum(h))
    diff=S-s
    if abs(diff) <= 1e-10:
        return fix_residual(h,S)
    # If z not far from [0,1] and can distribute, simple:
    if abs(diff) <= max(1e-6, 1e-4*n) and float(np.max(np.abs(z-h))) <= 0.5:
        # But if saturated, distribution okay
        if diff >0:
           room=1-h
           tot=room.sum()
           if tot>1e-12:
              h = h + diff*room/tot
              return fix_residual(h,S)
        else similar
    # robust bisection
```

```
def apply_move_corr(corr,h, i, j, delta):
    # change h_i += delta, h_j -= delta
    old_i = h[i]; old_j=h[j]
    # update corr using old h and b=1-h
    # For index a with change da
    b = 1-h
    # a=i da=delta
    # h_a as u: v=0..a, p=n-1+a-v. corr slice p[n-1 , n-1+a] reversed: corr[n-1:n-1+a+1] += delta * b[0:a+1][::-1]
    # b_a as v: u=a..n-1, p=n-1+d, d=0..n-1-a: corr[n-1:n-a? wait d from 0 to n-1-a => p n-1 to n-1+n-1-a = 2n-2-a] length n-a: += -delta * h[a:]
    # a=j da=-delta
    ...
    # cross if i!=j: weight = -delta_i*delta_j = delta^2? Add to corr[p where ordered pair (i,j)] and (j,i)
    if i != j:
       p_ij = n-1 + (i-j)
       p_ji = n-1 + (j-i)
       cross = -delta*(-delta) = delta*delta
       corr[p_ij] += cross
       corr[p_ji] += cross
```

Annealing schedule:
```
def anneal_exact(h,n,time_limit,seed=7,Tstart=0.005,Tend=1e-6):
   h = project_simplex(h, n/2).copy()
   c = exact_c5(h)
   best_h = h.copy(); best_c = c
   t0=time.time()
   rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
   # Estimate number of batches: use fixed batch=1000 or 2000
   batch = 2048 if n <= 90 else 512
   total_b = max(1, int(time_limit / 0.05))? Don't know per batch time. Use loop until time.
   T = Tstart
   cooldown = 0
   while time.time() - t0 < time_limit:
       for _ in range(batch):
          i = int(rng.integers(n)); j = int(rng.integers(n)); while j==i: j=int(rng.integers(n))
          hi=h[i]; hj=h[j]
          low = max(-hi, hj-1.0)
          high = min(1.0-hi, hj)
          if high - low < 1e-12: continue
          # Choose up to 2 deltas: uniform, and maybe small random near 0?
          for _m in range(1): # one for speed
             u = rng.random()
             d = low + u*(high-low)
             if abs(d) < 1e-13: continue
             # ensure not making out of bounds by epsilon
             d = max(low+1e-12, min(high-1e-12, d))
             old_i=hi; old_j=hj
             h[i]=old_i+d; h[j]=old_j-d
             cn = exact_c5(h)
             if cn < c - 1e-12 or (cn - c <= 0.02 and rng.random() < math.exp(-(cn-c)/T)):
                c=cn
                if c < best_c - 1e-12:
                   best_c=c; best_h=h.copy()
             else:
                h[i]=old_i; h[j]=old_j
       # cool
       T = max(Tend, T * 0.99) maybe. If batch time unknown, use per batch cooling.
       # if no improvement long, reset to best? We can if c > best_c and T small reset h=best copy
       if c > best_c + 0.01 and rng.random() < 0.05:
           h = best_h.copy(); c=best_c
       if time_check break
   return best_h,best_c
```

def make_starts(n, seed, parent, include_current_best, current_best_n, current_best_h):
  S=n/2; out=[]
  # add current best resampled if available
  if current_best_h is not None:
     h = resample(current_best_h, n) ... if exact C; add (0.0? just candidate)
  # add parent variants
  if parent is not None:
     base = resample(parent,n); base=project
     variants = [base, base[::-1].copy(), 1-base]
     for v: add; also noised with scale 0.01,0.02,0.05
  # random binary if even:
  rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
  if n%2==0:
    for k in range(3):
       x=np.zeros(n, dtype=float); idx=rng.permutation(n); x[idx[:n//2]]=1
       out.append(project(x,S))
  # random beatty/golden:
  for alpha in [0.381966, 0.414213, 0.5, 0.276]:
     vals = (np.arange(n)*alpha) % 1.0
     x = (vals < 0.5).astype(float)
     # adjust exact sum maybe by selecting top n/2 or project
     # project might alter pattern; but binary threshold maybe.
     order = np.argsort(vals)
     y=np.zeros(n); y[order[:int(S) if integer?]]? For S non-integer, set floor and fractional one? Simpler: y = (vals < 0.5).astype(float); y=project(y,S). Add.
  # random sine:
  for k in range(4):
     f1 = rng.choice([1,2,3,5]); f2 = rng.choice([2,3,5,7]); p1,p2=rng.random()*2π
     score = 0.5 + 0.4*np.sin(2π*f1*np.arange(n)/n + p1) + 0.2*np.sin(2π*f2*...)
     y = clip(score,0,1); y=project(y,S); add
  # random projected
  for k in range(4):
     y = rng.random(n); y = project(y,S); add
  # remove duplicates by rounded 3 decimals maybe.
```

def process_n(n, time_alloc, seed, parent, best_h, best_c, use_analytic):
   t0=time.time(); remaining=time_alloc
   scored = score_starts(...)
   if not scored: return
   # run smooth on top min(3, len(scored)) but ensure parent if not top? Actually best starts top.

       elapsed=time.time()-t0
       if elapsed > time_alloc - 15: break
       h0,c0=scored[rank]
       # give more time if rank 0 and c0 near best
       rem = time_alloc - elapsed
       sm_time = min(rem*0.4, 60 if n<=64 else 90)
       h1,c1 = smooth_optimize(h0,n,sm_time,use_analytic,seed)
       # if c1 not better than c0? We keep best; maybe smooth returned not improved, but still h1 maybe soft local. exact_refine could improve.
       rem = time_alloc - (time.time()-t0)

       rem = ...

          h3,c3 = anneal_exact(h2,n,an_time,seed+2)
       # update best after each
   # if still time, run anneal from best start longer
   ...
   return best_h,best_c

def ensure_feasible_from_input(arr):

def minimal_feasible(h,S):
   h_orig=np.clip(np.asarray(h,float),0,1)
   diff=S - sum(h_orig)
   if abs(diff) <=1e-12: return h_orig
   # find coordinates with room
   if diff>0:
      room=1-h_orig
      total_room=room.sum()
      if total_room >= diff:
         # add proportionally to room to all to smooth? Or one coordinate. Proportional okay.
         h_orig = h_orig + diff*room/total_room
         return clip fix
   else: mass = h_orig; total=mass.sum(); if total>=-diff: h_orig = h_orig + diff*mass/total
   # if not, project
```

Detailed helper implementations:

`exact_c5(h)`:
```
def exact_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return float('inf')
    # maybe ensure contiguous
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(corr) * (2.0 / n))
```
If h contains nan? no.

`fix_sum_clip(h,S)`:
```
def fix_sum_clip(h, S):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = S - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) <= 1e-13:
        return h
    if diff > 0:
       room = 1.0 - h
       tot = float(np.sum(room))
       if tot > 1e-14:
          # add proportionally
          h = h + diff * (room / tot)
          h = np.clip(h, 0, 1)
       # if residual
       diff2 = S - float(np.sum(h))
       if abs(diff2) > 1e-14:
          idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
          add = min(diff2, 1.0 - h[idx]) if diff2 > 0 else max(diff2, -h[idx])
          h[idx] += add
          h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    else:
       mass = h
       tot = float(np.sum(mass))
       if tot > 1e-14:
          h = h + diff * (mass / tot)
          h = np.clip(h,0,1)
       diff2 = S - float(np.sum(h))
       if abs(diff2) > 1e-14:
          idx = int(np.argmin(h))
          add = min(diff2, 1.0 - h[idx]) if diff2 > 0 else max(diff2, -h[idx])
          h[idx] += add
          h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
```
This proportional adjustment might overshoot if diff>total room? If infeasible due to S outside [0,n], not here. If diff big but after clipping still possible: if diff>sum(room), adding proportional exceeds room? Actually if diff <= total room, h_i + diff*room_i/tot <= h_i + room_i =1 if diff <= tot. Good. If diff > tot (impossible unless sum<0), clip and residual maybe unfixable, but S n/2 always feasible. If initial h clipped sum > S, diff negative, total mass >= -diff? if sum > S yes total mass = sum >S so diff = S-sum = - (sum-S); -diff = sum-S <= sum=tot. Good.
This is mild and O(n). Use it as `project_simplex_mild` when z already within bounds (all h clipped equals z and diff small). But for line search, z may be outside. We'll implement robust `project_simplex` with bisection for outside.

`project_simplex(z,S)`:
```
def project_simplex(z, S):
   z=np.asarray(z,float)
   n=len(z)
   if n==0: return z
   # if z already in [0,1] within tolerance and sum close, use mild fix
   if np.min(z) >= -1e-10 and np.max(z) <= 1+1e-10:
       return fix_sum_clip(z, S)
   # if near bounding and diff small? use mild after clipping? Might be okay if not far.
   hc = np.clip(z,0,1)
   diff = S - sum(hc)
   # if clipped values not far and simple can fix:
   if abs(diff) <= max(1e-4, 0.05*n) or True? Simpler try simple projection with clipping and proportional, if result has nonnegative and close, use. But simple after clipping may not be Euclidean but feasible. For large z, proportional distribution may be okay? It will set h=clip(z) and then adjust proportional to room/mass. This is feasible and inside. Could use always and skip bisection. But for gradient descent, using non-orthogonal projection may hurt. Yet line search uses small steps maybe. To be safe, use bisection if any z outside significantly.
   if np.max(np.abs(z - hc)) > 1e-3:
       return project_bisection(z,S)
   # if in bounds or close, use fast fix
   return fix_sum_clip(hc,S)
```
But if z outside by 0.5, fix_sum_clip only clips then adjusts; may not move z toward feasible as much as projection. E.g. z=[2,0,0,...], target sum S; projection would cap at 1 and distribute? Simple same: clip [1,0...] then fix diff. If diff negative? It may be okay. Use simple? To avoid bisection overhead, maybe always simple? But known projection? Let's keep bisection robust.

`project_bisection(z,S)`:
```
def project_bisection(z,S):
   z=np.asarray(z,float)
   n=len(z)
   lo = float(np.min(z)) - 1.0
   hi = float(np.max(z)) + 1.0
   # We need find theta. f(theta)=sum(clip(z-theta,0,1))-S decreasing.
   # If at lo sum>=S and hi sum<=S? yes because range wide.
   for _ in range(70):
      mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
      s=np.sum(np.clip(z-mid,0.0,1.0))
      if s > S:
         lo=mid
      else:
         hi=mid
   h=np.clip(z-0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)
   return fix_sum_clip(h,S)
```
70 iterations. For 1000 projections, 70k Python loops; okay.

But line search calls many. Could cache z? No.

`resample_h(h0,n)`:
```
def resample_h(h0, n):
   h0=np.asarray(h0,float)
   m=h0.size
   if m==n: return h0.copy()
   if m==1: return np.full(n, float(h0[0]))
   # endpoint=False? Use cell centers.
   x0=(np.arange(m)+0.5)*(2.0/m)
   x1=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
   return np.interp(x1,x0,h0)
```

def smooth_optimize(h,n,time_limit,use_analytic,seed):
    t0=time.time()
    if time_limit <= 0: return h, exact_c5(h)
    S=0.5*n
    h=project_simplex(h,S).copy()
    c=exact_c5(h)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c
    if time_limit < 25:
       return best_h,best_c
    T_list=[0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.004,0.001,0.0005]
    # if small time use [0.1,0.03,0.005]
    if time_limit < 60: T_list=[0.1,0.03,0.005]
    if time_limit < 25: T_list=[0.05,0.01]
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    per_t = time_limit / len(T_list)
    for T in T_list:
       if time.time()-t0 >= time_limit: break
       f=soft_objective(h,n,T)
       step=0.1
       stall=0
       tp_start=time.time()
       while time.time()-tp_start < per_t and time.time()-t0 < time_limit:
          g = smooth_grad_analytic if use... else fd
          gn=np.linalg.norm(g)
          if gn < 1e-12: break
          d = -g/gn
          best_f=f; best_cand=None; best_alpha=0.1
          bases=(0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5)
          # maybe add step variable? We can dynamic: bases + step
          for alpha in bases:
             cand=project_simplex(h + alpha*d, S)
             fc=soft_objective(cand,n,T)
             if fc < best_f - 1e-11:
                best_f=fc; best_cand=cand; best_alpha=alpha
          if best_cand is None:
             small=(0.001,0.002,1.0)
             for alpha in small:
                cand=project_simplex(h+alpha*d,S); fc=soft_objective(cand,n,T)
                if fc < best_f - 1e-11: ...
          if best_cand is not None:
             h=best_cand; f=best_f; stall=0
          else:
             stall += 1
             if stall % 10 == 0:
                # random small step
                alpha = 0.02
                direction = rng.normal(size=n)
                normdir=np.linalg.norm(direction)
                if normdir>0: direction=direction/normdir
                cand=project_simplex(h + alpha*direction, S)
                fc=soft_objective(cand,n,T)
                if fc < f:
                   h=cand; f=fc; stall=0
             if stall > 40: break
       c_new=exact_c5(h)
       if c_new < best_c - 1e-12:
          best_c=c_new; best_h=h.copy()
       # Also if h soft improved but exact not, maybe exact refine later.
    return best_h,best_c
```

  h_sm,c_sm,h_b,c_b = smooth_optimize(h0,n,smooth_time,use_analytic, seed+rank)
  if c_b < proc_c: proc_h=h_b.copy(); proc_c=c_b
  workspace_h = h_sm if c_sm <= proc_c + 0.02 else h_b
  # exact refine
  if rem>20:
     h_e,c_e,h_be,c_be = exact_refine4(workspace_h,...)
     if c_be < proc_c: ...
     workspace_h = h_e if c_e <= proc_c+0.02 else h_be
  # anneal
  if rem>30:
     h_a,c_a = anneal_exact4(workspace_h,...) # returns final,best
     if c_a < proc_c: ...
  # maybe final exact refine from local best
```
Need exact_refine return final and best? It already returns best exact; maybe workspace final not improved. We can just return best and final same? It can return final h and best c separately. Simpler: all optimization functions can return `(final_h, final_c, best_h, best_c)`. For anneal, final_h current, best_h. For exact_refine similar. Let's define unambiguous:
- `smooth_optimize` returns `(h_last, c_last, h_best, c_best)`.
- `exact_refine` returns `(h_last, c_last, h_best, c_best)`.
- `anneal_exact` returns `(h_last, c_last, h_best, c_best)`.
Where `h_last` is the state after function (maybe not best), `c_last` exact; `h_best,c_best` best found within function.
Then process updates with each `h_best,c_best`. For workspace, choose `h_last` if c_last close; else `h_best`.

In smooth, if no exact improvement, h_best=original, h_last=final soft state. Good.

Exact_refine can update both. Use T for gradient maybe 1e-4. It should track best exact (should be no less than start if accepts only improvements). Final h may be best if it accepts all. It can just return h,c,h,c.

Anneal returns final current and best (current may be worse than best). Good.

Let's write each precisely.

`exact_c5`:
```
def exact_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return float('inf')
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(corr) * (2.0 / n))
```

`exact_c5_fast` skip maybe.

`fix_sum_clip` as above. Need careful with diff sign and proportional addition. When diff>0 and total room >0, `h = h + diff * room / tot` may produce small >1 due to rounding; clip. Then residual fix. If diff<0, `h = h + diff * h / tot` (subtract proportionally). Good.

`project_bisection` with 70 iterations. If z min/max huge, 70 enough; maybe use 80. `fix_sum_clip` at end.

`project_simplex(z,S)`:
```
def project_simplex(z,S):
    z=np.asarray(z,dtype=float)
    n=z.size
    if n==0: return z
    if np.min(z) >= -1e-10 and np.max(z) <= 1.0+1e-10:
        return fix_sum_clip(z,S)
    hc=np.clip(z,0.0,1.0)
    diff=S - float(np.sum(hc))
    # If close enough, use fast feasible projection (orthogonal not exact)
    if abs(float(np.max(z) - np.max(hc))) <= 1e-3 and abs(float(np.min(z) - np.min(hc))) <= 1e-3:
        return fix_sum_clip(hc,S)
    return project_bisection(z,S)
```
But if z outside by 0.2, we go bisection. Good. For line search, outside small maybe fast. Bisection uses z possibly outside large. Fine.

`ensure_initial_feasible(arr)`:
```
def ensure_initial_feasible(arr):
   h=np.asarray(arr,dtype=float).copy()
   n=h.size; S=0.5*n
   # if already within tolerance, mild adjust
   clipped=np.clip(h,0,1)
   if np.array_equal? no
   if float(np.sum(np.abs(h - clipped))) <= 1e-10:
       h2=fix_sum_clip(clipped,S)
       # if sum error of original small and h2 close to original
       if float(np.sum(np.abs(h2 - h))) <= 1e-6:
           return h2
   return project_simplex(h,S)
```
But if original sum error 1e-5, h2 may change; okay.

`make_starts` top-level:
Need use `math.pi`. Avoid too many starts for large n? Our starts count <= maybe 25. For n=240, random and vector ops fine. Score exact for 25*240 maybe exact correlate length 479 ~ O(240^2) 57k*25=1.4M, okay.
```
def make_starts(n, seed, parent, current_best_h):
    S=0.5*n
    starts=[]
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    if current_best_h is not None:
       add_start(starts, resample_h(current_best_h,n), S)
    if parent is not None:
       p=resample_h(parent,n)
       base=project_simplex(p,S)
       add_start(starts, base, S)
       add_start(starts, base[::-1].copy(), S)
       add_start(starts, 1.0 - base, S)
       for sc in (0.005,0.02,0.05,0.1):
           noisy=base + sc*rng.standard_normal(n)
           add_start(starts,noisy,S)
    # binary if even
    if n%2==0 and n>=4:
       half=n//2
       for _ in range(3):
          x=np.zeros(n)
          idx=rng.permutation(n)
          x[idx[:half]]=1.0
          add_start(starts,x,S)
    # beatty
    alphas=(0.3819660112501051, 0.4142135623730951, 0.30901699437494745, 0.276393202250021)
    for alpha in alphas:
       vals=(np.arange(n)*alpha) % 1.0
       x=(vals < 0.5).astype(float)
       add_start(starts,x,S)
    # sine
    ts=np.arange(n)/float(max(1,n))
    for _ in range(6):
       f1=int(rng.integers(1,6)); f2=int(rng.integers(2,9))
       p1=float(rng.random())*2.0*math.pi; p2=float(rng.random())*2.0*math.pi
       score=0.5+0.35*np.sin(2.0*math.pi*f1*ts+p1)+0.25*np.sin(2.0*math.pi*f2*ts+p2)
       add_start(starts,score,S)
    # random
    for _ in range(4):
       add_start(starts, rng.random(n), S)
    return starts
```
For even binary, if sum S integer, project leaves binary exact? It may slight adjust if sum exact. Good. For odd, binary threshold sum floor/ceil, project will add fractional proportional to room? It will distribute mass over zeros proportionally, making many small fractional values; maybe okay. Could be undesirable. For binary starts when n odd, use project after setting floor half ones and one 0.5? But project simple modifies all zeros proportionally for 0.5 deficit, resulting many small fractions instead of one 0.5. Maybe bad. Define binary feasible for any n:
```
def binary_feasible(n, rng):
  x=np.zeros(n)
  half=int(n//2)
  idx=rng.permutation(n); x[idx[:half]]=1
  if n%2==1: x[idx[half]]=0.5
  return x
```
Sum exactly S. Use this for all n, 3 patterns. Replace binary section:
```
for _ in range(3):
   x=...
```
No need even condition. Good.

Beatty threshold for odd: We can also select exact floor + fractional one by choosing values? `add_start` project proportional. Could instead sort vals and assign: first floor ->1, next one -> fractional, rest 0. Let's implement helper? Top-level `threshold_binary_feasible(vals, S)`:
```
def threshold_feasible(vals,S):
  x=np.zeros_like(vals)
  k=int(math.floor(S)); frac=S-k
  order=np.argsort(vals)
  x[order[:k]]=1.0
  if frac>0: x[order[k]]=frac
  return x
```
Use for beatty/sine? For sine maybe project smoother. Use beatty with this to retain binary-ish. Need `order` O(n log n) small. Define helper top-level.

def score_starts(starts, top):

`soft_objective`:
```
def soft_objective(h,n,T):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    vals = (2.0/n)*np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    m = float(np.max(vals))
    if T <= 1e-12:
       return m
    shifted = (vals - m)/T
    # clip to avoid underflow; upper 0
    exp = np.exp(np.clip(shifted, -745.0, 0.0))
    s = float(np.sum(exp))
    if s <= 0.0:
       return m
    return float(m + T * math.log(s))
```
If vals contain nan? no.

`soft_grad_analytic` use weights nonzero. Need handle if `s<=0` all weights zero? fallback center/active.
```
def soft_grad_analytic(h,n,T):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    vals=(2.0/n)*np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    m=float(np.max(vals))
    weights=np.zeros(2*n-1)
    if T <=1e-12:
        active = vals >= m - 1e-12
        if not np.any(active): active[np.argmax(vals)]=True
        weights[active]=1.0/float(np.sum(active))
    else:
        shifted=(vals-m)/T
        exp=np.exp(np.clip(shifted,-745,0))
        s=float(np.sum(exp))
        if s>0: weights=exp/s
        else: weights[np.argmax(vals)]=1.0
    g=np.zeros(n)
    center=n-1
    # iterate nonzero weights
    idxs=np.nonzero(weights > 1e-10)[0]
    for p in idxs:
       w=float(weights[p])
       if p == center:
          g += w*(1.0 - 2.0*h)
       elif p < center:
          a = center - p
          # minus a
          g[:-a] += w*(1.0 - h[a:])
          g[a:] -= w*h[:-a]
       else:
          a = p - center
          # plus a
          g[a:] += w*(1.0 - h[:-a])
          g[:-a] -= w*h[a:]
    return g
```
Need note if a may equal n? p range 0..2n-2, center n-1, a up to n-1. For p<center, a= center-p between1 and n-1. Slices `g[:-a]` length n-a; for a=n-1 length1; `g[a:]` length1. For p>center similarly. Good.
For T large, weights all >1e-10 maybe all idxs. Fine.
Potential issue: For p<center minus a, term is corr[p] = sum h[j]*(1-h[j+a])? Wait p<center => d = p-center = -a: corr=Σ h[j]*(1-h[j+a]) (minus). Gradient: grad[j] += 1 - h[j+a], grad[j+a] -= h[j]. We used `g[:-a] += w*(1.0 - h[a:]); g[a:] -= w*h[:-a]`. Good.
For p>center plus a: corr=Σ h[j+a]*(1-h[j]); gradient `g[a:] += 1-h[:n-a]; g[:n-a] -= h[a:]`. Good.

`soft_grad_fd`:
```
def soft_grad_fd(h,n,T,eps=1e-5):
   h=np.asarray(h,float); g=np.zeros(n)
   for i in range(n):
      hp=h.copy(); hp[i]+=eps
      hm=h.copy(); hm[i]-=eps
      g[i]=(soft_objective(hp,n,T)-soft_objective(hm,n,T))/(2*eps)
   return g
```
If eps causes large outside bounds, okay. But for T small, soft_obj can have large derivatives due to max; finite diff with eps 1e-5 may be okay. Could use eps=1e-4? We'll set 1e-5. It will be slower but fallback rare.

`gradient_check`:
```
def gradient_check(n=13,T=0.05):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(98765)
    h=project_simplex(rng.random(n),0.5*n)
    g=soft_grad_analytic(h,n,T)
    eps=1e-5
    fd=np.zeros(n)
    for i in range(n):
      hp=h.copy(); hp[i]+=eps; fm=h.copy(); fm[i]-=eps
      fd[i]=(soft_objective(hp,n,T)-soft_objective(fm,n,T))/(2*eps)
    err=float(np.max(np.abs(g-fd)))
    scale=max(1.0,float(np.max(np.abs(fd))))
    return err <= 1e-3*scale
```
If h at bounds, h±eps outside; okay. Use n not too large.

`smooth_optimize` details:
```
def smooth_optimize(h,n,time_limit,use_analytic,seed):
    h=np.asarray(h,float).copy()
    S=0.5*n
    h=project_simplex(h,S)
    c=exact_c5(h)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c
    t0=time.time()
    if time_limit <= 0 or n<=1:
       return h,c,best_h,best_c
    if time_limit < 20:
       T_list=(0.1,0.03,0.005)
    elif time_limit < 60:
       T_list=(0.2,0.1,0.05,0.01)
    else:
       T_list=(0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.004,0.001,0.0005)
    per_t = time_limit / len(T_list)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    for T in T_list:
       if time.time()-t0 >= time_limit: break
       f=soft_objective(h,n,T)
       stall=0
       tp_start=time.time()
       while time.time()-tp_start < per_t and time.time()-t0 < time_limit:
           if use_analytic:
              g=soft_grad_analytic(h,n,T)
           else:
              g=soft_grad_fd(h,n,T)
           gn=float(np.linalg.norm(g))
           if gn < 1e-12: break
           d = -g/gn
           best_f=f; best_cand=None
           bases=(0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5)
           for alpha in bases:
              cand=project_simplex(h+alpha*d,S)
              fc=soft_objective(cand,n,T)
              if fc < best_f - 1e-11:
                 best_f=fc; best_cand=cand
           if best_cand is None:
              for alpha in (0.0008,0.001,1.0):
                 cand=project_simplex(h+alpha*d,S)
                 fc=soft_objective(cand,n,T)
                 if fc < best_f - 1e-11:
                    best_f=fc; best_cand=cand
           if best_cand is not None:
              h=best_cand; f=best_f; stall=0
           else:
              stall +=1
              if stall % 10 == 0:
                 direction = rng.normal(size=n)
                 dn=np.linalg.norm(direction)
                 if dn>0: direction=direction/dn
                 cand=project_simplex(h + 0.02*direction, S)
                 fc=soft_objective(cand,n,T)
                 if fc < f:
                    h=cand; f=fc; stall=0
              if stall > 50: break
       c_new=exact_c5(h)
       if c_new < best_c - 1e-12:
          best_c=c_new; best_h=h.copy()
    c_last=exact_c5(h)
    return h, c_last, best_h, best_c
```
Potential: `project_simplex` each candidate uses bisection maybe many. But ok.

`exact_refine`:
```
def exact_refine(h,n,time_limit,use_analytic,seed):
   h=np.asarray(h,float).copy(); S=0.5*n; h=project_simplex(h,S)
   c=exact_c5(h); best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c; t0=time.time()
   if time_limit<=0 or n<=1: return ...
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   stall=0
   while time.time()-t0<time_limit:
      if use_analytic: g=soft_grad_analytic(h,n,1e-4)
      else: g=soft_grad_fd(h,n,1e-4)
      gn=norm; if <... break
      d=-g/gn
      best_c=c; best_cand=h
      if use_analytic? no
      bases=(0.0005,0.001,0.003,0.008,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.25)
      for alpha in bases:
         cand=project_simplex(h+alpha*d,S)
         cc=exact_c5(cand)
         if cc < best_cand_c - 1e-12:
            best_cand_c=cc; best_cand=cand
      if best_cand_c < c -1e-12:
         h=best_cand; c=best_cand_c
         if c < best_c -1e-12: best...
         stall=0
      else:
         stall +=1
         if stall %10 ==0:
            direction=rng.normal(size=n); normalize
            aa=0.01 + 0.002*(stall%20)
            cand=project_simplex(h+aa*direction,S); cc=exact_c5(cand)
            if cc < c: accept
         if stall>150: break
   return h, exact_c5(h), best_h, best_c
```
Correct variable names.

`anneal_exact`:
```
def anneal_exact(h,n,time_limit,seed,Tstart=0.004,Tend=1e-6):
   h=project_simplex(h,S).copy(); c=exact_c5(h); best...
   t0; rng
   if Tstart <=0: Tstart=Tend*10
   # batch
   if n<=64: batch=2048
   elif n<=128: batch=1024
   else: batch=512
   while time.time()-t0 < time_limit:
      # T schedule
      frac = min(1.0, (time.time()-t0)/time_limit) if time_limit>0 else 1.0
      T = Tstart * (Tend/Tstart) ** frac
      for _ in range(batch):
          if time.time()-t0 >= time_limit: break? To not overshoot too much, check batch outer only. Batch maybe 2048, fine. But if time_limit small, batch can exceed; we can check every 256? Use inner modulo? Simpler: inside for, if `(_ & 255)==0 and time...` break; But bitwise okay. Or check every 128.
          i=int(rng.integers(n)); j=int((i+1+rng.integers(n-1))%n)
          hi=h[i]; hj=h[j]
          low = -hi if -hi > hj-1.0 else hj-1.0
          high = 1.0-hi if 1.0-hi < hj else hj
          if high - low < 1e-12: continue
          if rng.random() < 0.15:
             d = low if rng.random() < 0.5 else high
          else:
             d = low + rng.random()*(high-low)
          eps=1e-12
          if d <= low + eps:
             if high - low <= 2*eps: continue
             d = low + eps
          if d >= high - eps:
             if high - low <= 2*eps: continue
             d = high - eps
          old_i=hi; old_j=hj
          h[i]=hi+d; h[j]=hj-d
          cn=exact_c5(h)
          delta=cn-c
          accepted=False
          if delta < -1e-12:
             accepted=True
          else:
             if delta <= 0.02:
                if T > 1e-12:
                   arg=-delta/T
                   if arg > -700.0:
                      if rng.random() < math.exp(arg): accepted=True
                   else: accepted=False
                else:
                   accepted = delta <= 1e-12
          if accepted:
             c=cn
             if c < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c=c; best_h=h.copy()
          else:
             h[i]=old_i; h[j]=old_j
      # periodic recompute? Not with full recompute each proposal.
      # occasionally reset if too high
      if c > best_c + 0.01 and rng.random() < 0.005:
          h=best_h.copy(); c=best_c
   return h, exact_c5(h), best_h, best_c
```
If n=1, `rng.integers(n-1)` invalid; handle early return. If n<=1 return.

Could optimize `exact_c5` inside anneal by not using asarray: define `exact_c5_float(h)` but okay. Since h is ndarray contiguous. `np.correlate` on ndarray.

Potential bug: In anneal, h values after many accepted moves remain sum feasible (same delta add/subtract). But due to clipping d to bounds with eps, if d set not exactly low/high due eps, sum still preserved (hi+d, hj-d). Bounds maybe within 1e-12; final exact objective okay. At end, project mildly and compare.
In final run, after all, call `h_final=project_simplex(best_h, 0.5*best_n)`, c_final=exact_c5. If c_final < best_c? best; if c_final > best_c but best_h infeasible? We should return feasible; if projection worsens, might fail target. We can instead use `fix_sum_clip(best_h,S)` instead of full project to minimally adjust. def final_adjust(h,S):
   h=np.asarray(h,float)
   if np.min(h)>=-1e-8 and np.max(h)<=1+1e-8:
      return fix_sum_clip(h,S)

```

`process_n` implementation with four-tuple. Need be concise but correct.
```
def process_n(n,time_alloc,seed,parent,best_h,best_c,use_analytic):
    t0=time.time()
    S=0.5*n
    starts=make_starts(n,seed,parent,best_h)
    scored=score_starts(starts,12)
    if not scored:
       h=project_simplex(resample_h(best_h,n),S); c=exact_c5(h); return h,c
    # local best pick from scored min
    proc_h=scored[0][1].copy(); proc_c=scored[0][0]
    top_count = 3 if n <= 90 else 2
    # If parent current best? scored[0] likely. If global best is better than scored[0], add? Actually make_starts includes best resampled, so should be among. If global best n different, resampling may have c > global but still.
    for rank in range(min(top_count, len(scored))):
       elapsed = time.time()-t0
       rem = time_alloc - elapsed
       if rem < 10: break
       h0,c0 = scored[rank]
       frac = 0.35 if rank == 0 else 0.25
       sm_t = min(rem*frac, 120.0)
       if sm_t > 5:
          h_sm, c_sm, h_b, c_b = smooth_optimize(h0,n,sm_t,use_analytic,seed+rank*17)

          # choose workspace

              workspace = h_sm
          else:
              workspace = h_b
          elapsed = time.time()-t0; rem = time_alloc-elapsed
          if rem > 20:
             er_t = min(rem*0.35, 60.0)
             if er_t > 5:
                h_e,c_e,h_be,c_be=exact_refine(workspace,n,er_t,use_analytic,seed+100+rank)
                if c_be < proc_c: ...
                workspace = h_e if c_e <= proc_c+0.02 else h_be
          elapsed = time.time()-t0; rem = time_alloc-elapsed
          if rem > 30 and n <= 180:
             an_t = min(rem*0.5, 150.0 if n<=90 else 200.0)
             if an_t > 5:
                h_a,c_a,h_ba,c_ba=anneal_exact(workspace,n,an_t,seed+200+rank)
                if c_ba < proc_c: ...
                workspace = h_a if c_a <= proc_c+0.02 else h_ba
    # If still time, final long anneal from proc_h
    rem = time_alloc - (time.time()-t0)
    if rem > 30 and n <= 180:
       an_t = min(rem*0.8, 300.0 if n<=90 else 200.0)
       h_a,c_a,h_ba,c_ba=anneal_exact(proc_h,n,an_t,seed+500)
       if c_ba < proc_c: ...
    return proc_h, proc_c

```
def fix_sum_clip(h,S):
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   diff=S-sum
   if abs(diff)<=1e-13: return h
   if diff>0:
      room=1-h
      idx=np.argmax(room)
      if room[idx] >= diff:
         h[idx]+=diff
      else:
         # distribute among all with room maybe proportional
         tot=room.sum()
         if tot>0: h += diff*room/tot
   else:
      mass=h
      idx=np.argmin(h)
      if mass[idx] >= -diff:
         h[idx]+=diff
      else:
         tot=mass.sum()
         if tot>0: h += diff*mass/tot
   return clip fix residual
```

`fix_sum_clip` code:
```
def fix_sum_clip(h, S):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(5):
        diff = S - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            if diff <= 0.02:
                idx = int(np.argmax(room))
                if room[idx] >= diff - 1e-14:
                    h[idx] += diff
                    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
                    continue
            tot = float(np.sum(room))
            if tot > 1e-14:
                h = h + diff * (room / tot)
            else:
                idx = int(np.argmax(room))
                h[idx] += diff
        else:
            mass = h
            if -diff <= 0.02:
                idx = int(np.argmin(h))
                if mass[idx] >= -diff - 1e-14:
                    h[idx] += diff
                    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
                    continue
            tot = float(np.sum(mass))
            if tot > 1e-14:
                h = h + diff * (mass / tot)
            else:
                idx = int(np.argmin(h))
                h[idx] += diff
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h
```
If diff>0 but tot=0 (all h=1) impossible because S=n/2<n for n>0. If n=1 S=0.5 and h maybe 1? diff=-0.5, mass tot 1 okay.
One issue: If diff > 0.02 and proportional adds small over some h_i >1? We check diff≤total room ensures no >1? If h_i room_i, add diff*room_i/tot; since diff/tot ≤1 (because diff ≤ total room if S≤n? Need prove: after clip sum s, diff=S-s. total room=n-s. Need diff≤n-s => S-s≤n-s => S≤n, true). So no overshoot. Similarly subtract diff*tot mass? diff negative, magnitude s-S. mass_i/tot*|diff| ≤ mass_i if |diff|≤tot=s. s-S≤s => S≥0. true. Good.

In the small diff one-coordinate branch, if room[idx] < diff, we fall to proportional but note diff>0.02? If diff<=0.02 but max room < diff (many tiny rooms), proportional diff/tot ≤1 still safe.

def ensure_initial_feasible(arr):
   h=np.asarray(arr,float)
   n=h.size; S=0.5*n
   if n==0: return np.array([])
   clipped=np.clip(h,0,1)
   diff=M - sum(clipped)
   if abs(diff) <= 1e-7 and float(np.sum(np.abs(h-clipped))) <= 1e-8:
       return fix_sum_clip(clipped,S)
   return project_simplex(h,S)

`threshold_feasible`:
```
def threshold_feasible(values, S):
    x=np.zeros_like(values, dtype=float)
    k=int(math.floor(S))
    frac=S-k
    if x.size == 0: return x
    order=np.argsort(values)
    if k>0: x[order[:k]]=1.0
    if frac > 1e-12 and k < x.size:
        x[order[k]] = frac
    return x
```

def process_n(n, time_alloc, seed, parent, best_h, best_c, use_analytic):
    t0 = time.time()

    if time_alloc <= 0:
       h = project_simplex(resample_h(best_h,n), S)
       return h, exact_c5(h)

    if not scored:

    proc_h = scored[0][1].copy()
    proc_c = scored[0][0]
    top_count=3 if n<=90 else 2
    for rank in range(min(top_count, len(scored))):
       elapsed=time.time()-t0
       rem=time_alloc-elapsed
       if rem < 10: break

          if c_b < proc_c - 1e-12:

          elapsed=time.time()-t0; rem=time_alloc-elapsed

Implement `project_simplex(z,S)`:
```
def project_simplex(z,S):
   z=np.asarray(z,float).ravel()
   n=z.size
   if n==0: return z
   # if z already feasible and sum close, quick
   h0=np.clip(z,0,1)
   s0=float(np.sum(h0))
   if abs(s0 - S) <= 1e-12 and np.min(z) >= -1e-12 and np.max(z) <= 1+1e-12:
       return fix_sum_clip(h0,S)
   lo=float(np.min(z)) - 1.0
   hi=float(np.max(z)) + 1.0
   for _ in range(50):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       s=np.sum(np.clip(z-mid,0.0,1.0))
       if s > S:
          lo=mid
       else:
          hi=mid
   h=np.clip(z-0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)
   return fix_sum_clip(h,S)
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math


def exact_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(corr) * (2.0 / n))


def fix_sum_clip(h, S):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel().copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(5):
        diff = S - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            if diff <= 0.02:
                idx = int(np.argmax(room))
                if room[idx] >= diff - 1e-14:
                    h[idx] += diff
                    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
                    continue
            tot = float(np.sum(room))
            if tot > 1e-14:
                h = h + diff * (room / tot)
            else:
                idx = int(np.argmax(room))
                h[idx] += diff
        else:
            mass = h
            if -diff <= 0.02:
                idx = int(np.argmin(h))
                if mass[idx] >= -diff - 1e-14:
                    h[idx] += diff
                    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
                    continue
            tot = float(np.sum(mass))
            if tot > 1e-14:
                h = h + diff * (mass / tot)
            else:
                idx = int(np.argmin(h))
                h[idx] += diff
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def project_simplex(z, S):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = z.size
    if n == 0:
        return z

    h0 = np.clip(z, 0.0, 1.0)
    s0 = float(np.sum(h0))

    if float(np.min(z)) >= -1e-10 and float(np.max(z)) <= 1.0 + 1e-10:
        diff = S - s0
        step = diff / float(n)
        h = h0 + step
        if float(np.min(h)) >= -1e-12 and float(np.max(h)) <= 1.0 + 1e-12:
            return fix_sum_clip(h, S)

    lo = float(np.min(z)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(z)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(45):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.sum(np.clip(z - mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if s > S:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    h = np.clip(z - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    return fix_sum_clip(h, S)


def ensure_initial_feasible(arr):
    h = np.asarray(arr, dtype=float).ravel()
    if h.size == 0:
        return h
    S = 0.5 * float(h.size)
    return project_simplex(h, S)


def resample_h(h0, n):
    h0 = np.asarray(h0, dtype=float).ravel()
    m = h0.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float)
    if m == n:
        return h0.copy()
    if m == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(h0[0]), dtype=float)
    x0 = (np.arange(m) + 0.5) * (2.0 / m)
    x1 = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    return np.interp(x1, x0, h0)


def first_min_key(item):
    return item[0]


def threshold_feasible(values, S):
    x = np.zeros_like(values, dtype=float)
    k = int(math.floor(S))
    frac = S - k
    if x.size == 0:
        return x
    order = np.argsort(values)
    if k > 0:
        x[order[:k]] = 1.0
    if frac > 1e-12 and k < x.size:
        x[order[k]] = frac
    return x


def add_start(starts, h, S):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel()
    if h.size == 0:
        return
    h = project_simplex(h, S)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        return
    starts.append(h.copy())


def make_starts(n, seed, parent, current_best_h):
    S = 0.5 * float(n)
    starts = []
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    if current_best_h is not None:
        add_start(starts, resample_h(current_best_h, n), S)

    if parent is not None:
        base_p = resample_h(parent, n)
        base = project_simplex(base_p, S)
        add_start(starts, base, S)
        add_start(starts, base[::-1].copy(), S)
        add_start(starts, 1.0 - base, S)
        for sc in (0.005, 0.02, 0.05, 0.10):
            noisy = base + sc * rng.standard_normal(n)
            add_start(starts, noisy, S)

    half = int(n // 2)
    for _ in range(3):
        x = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        idx = rng.permutation(n)
        if half > 0:
            x[idx[:half]] = 1.0
        if n % 2 == 1 and half < n:
            x[idx[half]] = 0.5
        add_start(starts, x, S)

    alphas = (
        0.3819660112501051,
        0.4142135623730951,
        0.30901699437494745,
        0.2763932022500210,
    )
    for alpha in alphas:
        vals = (np.arange(n) * alpha) % 1.0
        x = threshold_feasible(vals, S)
        add_start(starts, x, S)

    t = np.arange(n) / float(max(1, n))
    for _ in range(6):
        f1 = int(rng.integers(1, 6))
        f2 = int(rng.integers(2, 9))
        p1 = float(rng.random()) * 2.0 * math.pi
        p2 = float(rng.random()) * 2.0 * math.pi
        score = 0.5 + 0.35 * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * f1 * t + p1) + 0.25 * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * f2 * t + p2)
        add_start(starts, score, S)

    for _ in range(4):
        add_start(starts, rng.random(n), S)

    return starts


def score_starts(starts, top):
    scored = []
    seen = set()
    for h in starts:
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
            continue
        key = np.round(h, 5).tobytes()
        if key in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(key)
        c = exact_c5(h)
        scored.append((c, h))
    scored.sort(key=first_min_key)
    if top is None:
        return scored
    return scored[:top]


def soft_objective(h, n, T):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    vals = (2.0 / n) * np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    m = float(np.max(vals))
    if T <= 1e-12:
        return m
    shifted = (vals - m) / T
    exp = np.exp(np.clip(shifted, -745.0, 0.0))
    s = float(np.sum(exp))
    if s <= 0.0:
        return m
    return float(m + T * math.log(s))


def soft_grad_analytic(h, n, T):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return h
    vals = (2.0 / n) * np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    m = float(np.max(vals))
    weights = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)

    if T <= 1e-12:
        active = vals >= (m - 1e-12)
        if not np.any(active):
            active = np.zeros(vals.shape, dtype=bool)
            active[int(np.argmax(vals))] = True
        weights[active] = 1.0 / float(np.sum(active))
    else:
        shifted = (vals - m) / T
        exp = np.exp(np.clip(shifted, -745.0, 0.0))
        s = float(np.sum(exp))
        if s > 0.0:
            weights = exp / s
        else:
            weights[int(np.argmax(vals))] = 1.0

    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    center = n - 1
    idxs = np.nonzero(weights > 1e-10)[0]

    for p in idxs:
        w = float(weights[p])
        if p == center:
            g += w * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
        elif p < center:
            a = center - p
            g[:-a] += w * (1.0 - h[a:])
            g[a:] -= w * h[:-a]
        else:
            a = p - center
            g[a:] += w * (1.0 - h[:-a])
            g[:-a] -= w * h[a:]

    g *= (2.0 / n)
    return g


def soft_grad_fd(h, n, T, eps=1e-5):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = h.size
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if n == 0:
        return g
    for i in range(n):
        hp = h.copy()
        hm = h.copy()
        hp[i] += eps
        hm[i] -= eps
        g[i] = (soft_objective(hp, n, T) - soft_objective(hm, n, T)) / (2.0 * eps)
    return g


def gradient_check(n=13, T=0.05):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(98765)
    h = project_simplex(rng.random(n), 0.5 * float(n))
    g = soft_grad_analytic(h, n, T)
    eps = 1e-5
    fd = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for i in range(n):
        hp = h.copy()
        hm = h.copy()
        hp[i] += eps
        hm[i] -= eps
        fd[i] = (soft_objective(hp, n, T) - soft_objective(hm, n, T)) / (2.0 * eps)
    err = float(np.max(np.abs(g - fd)))
    scale = max(1.0, float(np.max(np.abs(fd))))
    return err <= 1e-3 * scale


def smooth_optimize(h, n, time_limit, use_analytic, seed):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel().copy()
    n = h.size
    if n <= 1 or time_limit <= 0.0:
        c = exact_c5(h)
        return h, c, h.copy(), c

    S = 0.5 * float(n)
    h = project_simplex(h, S)
    c = exact_c5(h)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c
    t0 = time.time()

    if time_limit < 20.0:
        T_list = (0.10, 0.03, 0.005)
    elif time_limit < 60.0:
        T_list = (0.20, 0.10, 0.05, 0.01)
    else:
        T_list = (0.20, 0.10, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.004, 0.001, 0.0005)

    per_t = time_limit / float(len(T_list))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    for T in T_list:
        if time.time() - t0 >= time_limit:
            break
        f = soft_objective(h, n, T)
        stall = 0
        tp_start = time.time()

        while time.time() - tp_start < per_t and time.time() - t0 < time_limit:
            if use_analytic:
                g = soft_grad_analytic(h, n, T)
            else:
                g = soft_grad_fd(h, n, T)

            gn = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
            if gn < 1e-12:
                break

            d = -g / gn
            best_f = f
            best_cand = None

            bases = (0.002, 0.005, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.50)
            for alpha in bases:
                cand = project_simplex(h + alpha * d, S)
                fc = soft_objective(cand, n, T)
                if fc < best_f - 1e-11:
                    best_f = fc
                    best_cand = cand

            if best_cand is None:
                for alpha in (0.0008, 0.001, 1.0):
                    cand = project_simplex(h + alpha * d, S)
                    fc = soft_objective(cand, n, T)
                    if fc < best_f - 1e-11:
                        best_f = fc
                        best_cand = cand

            if best_cand is not None:
                h = best_cand
                f = best_f
                stall = 0
            else:
                stall += 1
                if stall % 10 == 0:
                    direction = rng.normal(size=n)
                    dn = float(np.linalg.norm(direction))
                    if dn > 0.0:
                        direction = direction / dn
                        cand = project_simplex(h + 0.02 * direction, S)
                        fc = soft_objective(cand, n, T)
                        if fc < f:
                            h = cand
                            f = fc
                            stall = 0
                if stall > 50:
                    break

        c_new = exact_c5(h)
        if c_new < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c = c_new
            best_h = h.copy()

    c_last = exact_c5(h)
    return h, c_last, best_h, best_c


def exact_refine(h, n, time_limit, use_analytic, seed):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel().copy()
    n = h.size
    if n <= 1 or time_limit <= 0.0:
        c = exact_c5(h)
        return h, c, h.copy(), c

    S = 0.5 * float(n)
    h = project_simplex(h, S)
    c = exact_c5(h)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c
    t0 = time.time()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    stall = 0

    while time.time() - t0 < time_limit:
        if use_analytic:
            g = soft_grad_analytic(h, n, 1e-4)
        else:
            g = soft_grad_fd(h, n, 1e-4)

        gn = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        if gn < 1e-12:
            break

        d = -g / gn
        best_cand_c = c
        best_cand = h

        bases = (0.0005, 0.001, 0.003, 0.008, 0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.25)
        for alpha in bases:
            cand = project_simplex(h + alpha * d, S)
            cc = exact_c5(cand)
            if cc < best_cand_c - 1e-12:
                best_cand_c = cc
                best_cand = cand

        if best_cand_c < c - 1e-12:
            h = best_cand
            c = best_cand_c
            if c < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c = c
                best_h = h.copy()
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall % 10 == 0:
                direction = rng.normal(size=n)
                dn = float(np.linalg.norm(direction))
                if dn > 0.0:
                    direction = direction / dn
                    aa = 0.01 + 0.002 * float(stall % 20)
                    cand = project_simplex(h + aa * direction, S)
                    cc = exact_c5(cand)
                    if cc < c - 1e-12:
                        h = cand
                        c = cc
                        if c < best_c - 1e-12:
                            best_c = c
                            best_h = h.copy()
                        stall = 0
            if stall > 150:
                break

    c_last = exact_c5(h)
    return h, c_last, best_h, best_c


def anneal_exact(h, n, time_limit, seed, Tstart=0.003, Tend=1e-6):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel().copy()
    n = h.size
    if n <= 1 or time_limit <= 0.0:
        c = exact_c5(h)
        return h, c, h.copy(), c

    S = 0.5 * float(n)
    h = project_simplex(h, S)
    c = exact_c5(h)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c
    t0 = time.time()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    if n <= 64:
        batch = 2048
    elif n <= 128:
        batch = 1024
    else:
        batch = 512

    while time.time() - t0 < time_limit:
        frac = min(1.0, (time.time() - t0) / float(time_limit) if time_limit > 0 else 1.0)
        T = Tstart * pow(Tend / Tstart, frac)

        for step in range(batch):
            if (step % 256 == 0) and (time.time() - t0 >= time_limit):
                break

            i = int(rng.integers(n))
            j = int((i + 1 + rng.integers(max(1, n - 1))) % n)

            hi = float(h[i])
            hj = float(h[j])

            low = max(-hi, hj - 1.0)
            high = min(1.0 - hi, hj)
            if high - low < 1e-12:
                continue

            if rng.random() < 0.15:
                d = low if rng.random() < 0.5 else high
            else:
                d = low + rng.random() * (high - low)

            eps = 1e-12
            if high - low <= 2.0 * eps:
                continue
            d = min(max(d, low + eps), high - eps)
            if abs(d) <= 1e-13:
                continue

            old_i = h[i]
            old_j = h[j]
            h[i] = hi + d
            h[j] = hj - d

            cn = exact_c5(h)
            delta = cn - c
            accepted = False

            if delta < -1e-12:
                accepted = True
            else:
                if delta <= 0.02 and T > 1e-12:
                    arg = -delta / T
                    if arg > -700.0:
                        if rng.random() < math.exp(arg):
                            accepted = True
                    else:
                        accepted = False

            if accepted:
                c = cn
                if c < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_c = c
                    best_h = h.copy()
            else:
                h[i] = old_i
                h[j] = old_j

        if c > best_c + 0.01 and rng.random() < 0.005:
            h = best_h.copy()
            c = best_c

    c_last = exact_c5(h)
    return h, c_last, best_h, best_c


def process_n(n, time_alloc, seed, parent, best_h, best_c, use_analytic):
    t0 = time.time()
    S = 0.5 * float(n)

    if time_alloc <= 0.0:
        h = project_simplex(resample_h(best_h, n), S)
        return h, exact_c5(h)

    starts = make_starts(n, seed, parent, best_h)
    scored = score_starts(starts, 12)

    if not scored:
        h = project_simplex(resample_h(best_h, n), S)
        return h, exact_c5(h)

    proc_h = scored[0][1].copy()
    proc_c = scored[0][0]

    top_count = 3 if n <= 90 else 2
    for rank in range(min(top_count, len(scored))):
        elapsed = time.time() - t0
        rem = time_alloc - elapsed
        if rem < 10.0:
            break

        h0, c0 = scored[rank]
        frac = 0.35 if rank == 0 else 0.25
        sm_t = min(rem * frac, 120.0)

        if sm_t > 5.0:
            h_sm, c_sm, h_b, c_b = smooth_optimize(h0, n, sm_t, use_analytic, seed + rank * 17)
            if c_b < proc_c - 1e-12:
                proc_h = h_b.copy()
                proc_c = c_b
            workspace = h_sm if c_sm <= proc_c + 0.02 else h_b

            rem = time_alloc - (time.time() - t0)
            if rem > 20.0:
                er_t = min(rem * 0.35, 60.0)
                if er_t > 5.0:
                    h_e, c_e, h_be, c_be = exact_refine(workspace, n, er_t, use_analytic, seed + 100 + rank)
                    if c_be < proc_c - 1e-12:
                        proc_h = h_be.copy()
                        proc_c = c_be
                    workspace = h_e if c_e <= proc_c + 0.02 else h_be

            rem = time_alloc - (time.time() - t0)
            if rem > 30.0 and n <= 180:
                an_cap = 150.0 if n <= 90 else 200.0
                an_t = min(rem * 0.5, an_cap)
                if an_t > 5.0:
                    h_a, c_a, h_ba, c_ba = anneal_exact(workspace, n, an_t, seed + 200 + rank)
                    if c_ba < proc_c - 1e-12:
                        proc_h = h_ba.copy()
                        proc_c = c_ba
                    workspace = h_a if c_a <= proc_c + 0.02 else h_ba

    rem = time_alloc - (time.time() - t0)
    if rem > 30.0 and n <= 180:
        an_cap = 300.0 if n <= 90 else 200.0
        an_t = min(rem * 0.8, an_cap)
        if an_t > 5.0:
            h_a, c_a, h_ba, c_ba = anneal_exact(proc_h, n, an_t, seed + 500)
            if c_ba < proc_c - 1e-12:
                proc_h = h_ba.copy()
                proc_c = c_ba

    return proc_h, proc_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start_time = time.time()
    budget_s = max(5.0, float(budget_s))

    parent = globals().get("initial_h_values", None)
    parent_arr = None
    if parent is not None:
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(parent, dtype=float).ravel()
            if arr.size > 0:
                parent_arr = ensure_initial_feasible(arr)
        except Exception:
            parent_arr = None

    if parent_arr is not None and parent_arr.size > 0:
        best_h = parent_arr.copy()
        best_c = exact_c5(best_h)
    else:
        n0 = 45
        best_h = project_simplex(np.full(n0, 0.5, dtype=float), 0.5 * float(n0))
        best_c = exact_c5(best_h)

    use_analytic = bool(gradient_check()) if (budget_s - (time.time() - start_time)) > 5.0 else True

    parent_n = int(best_h.size) if parent_arr is not None else 45

    scale = max(0.10, min(1.0, budget_s / 1300.0))
    planned_base = []

    planned_base.append((parent_n, 260.0))
    if parent_n > 2:
        planned_base.append((parent_n - 1, 70.0))
    planned_base.append((parent_n + 1, 70.0))

    extra = [
        (45, 120.0),
        (90, 180.0),
        (64, 80.0),
        (135, 110.0),
        (180, 90.0),
        (240, 60.0),
    ]
    planned_base.extend(extra)

    allocs = {}
    order = []
    for n, base in planned_base:
        n = int(n)
        if n <= 1:
            continue
        alloc = max(5.0, base * scale)
        if n not in allocs:
            allocs[n] = alloc
            order.append(n)
        else:
            allocs[n] = max(allocs[n], alloc)

    reserve = max(30.0, min(200.0, 0.15 * budget_s))

    for idx, n in enumerate(order):
        elapsed = time.time() - start_time
        if budget_s - elapsed <= reserve + 10.0:
            break
        rem_available = budget_s - reserve - 10.0 - elapsed
        alloc_use = min(allocs[n], max(5.0, rem_available))
        if alloc_use < 5.0:
            continue

        try:
            cand_h, cand_c = process_n(
                n,
                alloc_use,
                int(seed) + 9000 + idx * 101,
                parent_arr,
                best_h,
                best_c,
                use_analytic,
            )
        except Exception:
            continue

        cand_h = np.asarray(cand_h, dtype=float).ravel()
        if cand_h.size == n and finite_ok(cand_h) and cand_c < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_h = cand_h.copy()
            best_c = float(cand_c)

    elapsed = time.time() - start_time
    final_budget = max(0.0, budget_s - elapsed - 1.0)
    if final_budget > 20.0 and best_h.size <= 180:
        ref_t = min(final_budget * 0.40, 120.0)
        if ref_t > 10.0:
            try:
                h_f, c_f, h_bf, c_bf = exact_refine(
                    best_h,
                    int(best_h.size),
                    ref_t,
                    use_analytic,
                    int(seed) + 7777,
                )
                if c_bf < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_h = h_bf.copy()
                    best_c = float(c_bf)
            except Exception:
                pass

        elapsed = time.time() - start_time
        an_t = max(0.0, min(final_budget * 0.60, budget_s - elapsed - 1.0, 300.0 if best_h.size <= 90 else 200.0))
        if an_t > 10.0:
            try:
                h_a, c_a, h_ba, c_ba = anneal_exact(
                    best_h,
                    int(best_h.size),
                    an_t,
                    int(seed) + 8888,
                )
                if c_ba < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_h = h_ba.copy()
                    best_c = float(c_ba)
            except Exception:
                pass

    best_h = np.asarray(best_h, dtype=float).ravel()
    S = 0.5 * float(best_h.size)
    old_c = exact_c5(best_h) if best_h.size > 0 else float("inf")
    try:
        h2 = project_simplex(best_h, S)
        c2 = exact_c5(h2)
        if finite_ok(h2) and c2 <= old_c + 1e-9:
            best_h = h2
            best_c = float(c2)
        else:
            best_c = float(old_c)
    except Exception:
        best_c = float(old_c)

    best_h = np.asarray(best_h, dtype=float).ravel()
    if best_h.size == 0:
        best_h = project_simplex(np.full(45, 0.5, dtype=float), 22.5)
        best_c = exact_c5(best_h)

    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_h.size)


def finite_ok(h):
    try:
        h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel()
        if h.size == 0:
            return False
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
            return False
        if np.min(h) < -1e-8 or np.max(h) > 1.0 + 1e-8:
            return False
        return True
    except Exception:
        return False
```
